\mbox{\hyperlink{compress_8c_a788dffccbc0311102d8967c19ef9ab00}{Z\+S\+T\+D\+\_\+adjust\+C\+Params()}} \+: optimize {\ttfamily c\+Par} for a given input ({\ttfamily src\+Size} and {\ttfamily dict\+Size}). mostly downsizing to reduce memory consumption and initialization. Both {\ttfamily src\+Size} and {\ttfamily dict\+Size} are optional (use 0 if unknown), but if both are 0, no optimization can be done. Note \+: c\+Par is considered validated at this stage. Use Z\+S\+T\+D\+\_\+check\+Params() to ensure that. Here is the call graph for this function\+:
